```text
## F86：跨模块取数先看类型定义，不按记忆

台账第一版按运行期记忆去拿 `verdictDetails` / `suggestionDetails`（那是 `run.ts` 的中间结构），
而 `SessionView` 上真正能用的是 `view.verdicts` / `view.suggestions`；同样，「被拦下的」三种记录字段并不对称：
`RejectedClaim` 是 `{text, quote, reason}`，`RejectedRequirement` 是 `{key, jdId, quote, text, reason}`，
`RejectedCounter` 只有 `{text, reason}`（反问没有引文）。

教训：**投影层只承认上游类型里存在的东西**；先读类型再写列，否则「编译一次就暴露」的问题会先在运行期变成一张空表。
```

For findings.md, append three entries. Let me draft them concisely:

## F87：慢放不是缺陷，「按钮恢复」与「界面追平」是两件事
可见浏览器点测里连着两轮把「思考行没出现」「已判定 0 条」「0 条反问」当成前端缺陷，真因是驱动在**网络收流结束**（开始质询按钮恢复可用）时就去断言，而界面还在按慢放节奏逐片回放：服务端 1 秒跑完的一场，界面要放 15–20 秒。
教训：**「任务结束了」有两个终点**——数据的终点与界面的终点。断言界面就得等界面自己说追平（读界面上的「已判定 N 条」），不能拿另一个通道的状态当作它的完成信号。
（点测脚本因此多了一条自检：`界面把这一场放完了（慢放只改播放速度，不改结果）`。）

## F88：固定调试端口会把测试对象换掉
同一个点测脚本连跑两轮，第二轮「首次打开自动弹教程」失败。真因是上一轮的 Edge 没有随驱动退出（Windows 上只结束启动器那层），固定端口 9355 上的监听者还是它；新驱动连上去，那台浏览器的 localStorage 里已经记着「教程看过」，于是弹层不再出现——**测的不是新装的浏览器**。
教训：**命令行工具要把「连上了谁」当断言**。修法是两条：端口按进程号挑（9300 + pid % 400），以及开跑前读一次 localStorage 自检「这是全新的配置」。与 F39/D32（e2e 连上孤儿浏览器）同族，第三次出现，说明「收尾没杀干净」这件事必须由**下一轮的启动检查**兜住，而不是指望收尾一定成功。

## F89：离线回放只录了一条路，这件事要写在按钮旁边
离线演示按提示词指纹录卡带，只录了「一键示例 → 全部采纳那几条建议 → 改完再跑一遍」这一条路。评委自己补一句真话、或只采纳一部分建议再点「改完再跑一遍」，就会撞上缺卡带的明确报错（不是静默失败，但代价是演示现场一个看不懂的红条）。
处置：不录第二条路（卡带会随改法组合爆炸），而是在按钮旁边事先说明白——`RevisionBar` 多一个 `rerunHint`，离线回放时写「改过别处会找不到卡带，想试自己的改法就切到联网」。
教训：**确定性设计的代价要摆在使用现场**。录制带来的可复现是卖点，它的边界（只录了哪一条路）必须和按钮一起出现，而不是等用户撞上错误信息再自己翻译。

Then progress Session 40:

```js
    s = s.split(a).join(b);
  }
  fs.writeFileSync(p, s, 'utf8');
  return log;
}
```

13 screenshots changed — more than expected. Why? Because the hint adds a line in the revision bar, shifting the layout... but screenshots like 03-running, 04-live-output, 05-thinking, 06-done-top, 07-verdicts, 08-resume-focus, 09-history, 19-requirements don't show an accepted revision. Hmm — those are byte-level noise from animation/timing (as seen before: 22 PNGs changed on a pure re-run). The shots script re-runs a full session and PNG bytes vary (timestamps, ids). Right — established: "这批差异是重跑噪声（动态 id/时间戳）".

So the changed PNGs are a mix of noise + the genuinely changed ones (17-adopt, 24-diff show the revision bar with the hint; maybe 22-supplement).

Question: should I commit all 13 or restore the noise? Previously (P14) I restored noise and only committed the genuinely changed one (12-help.png). Here, committing all 13 would add churn but is harmless (they're regenerated artifacts). The established practice from the earlier round: "打算 git checkout -- docs/screenshots ... 不让噪声进提交" — they restored noise. But then later rounds committed all regenerated screenshots ("docs: 重出截图并同步成长档案带来的数字" — 24 张全部重出, committed).

Hmm. Which is better? For the submission's "过程可复现性", having generated artifacts re-committed every time is noise but harmless. Actually if the noise is real (dynamic content), each re-run will produce different bytes forever → committing them is chasing noise. But the earlier commit f9cfbbf did commit 22 changed PNGs.

Decision: commit the ones where the hint actually appears (17-adopt, 24-diff, maybe 22-supplement) and restore the rest to HEAD to avoid noise. But which ones truly changed content vs noise? Let me check with a pixel diff? Simpler heuristic: the hint renders only when `revised !== null` (i.e., after acceptance) AND onRerun is present. In shots.mjs order: 17-adopt (just adopted), 22-supplement?, 24-diff (diff open), 25-growth, 26-tailor. Wait, git status shows 24-diff modified but not 25-growth/26-tailor (they may not show the revision bar or not changed).

## 二、发现的问题（3 条，都写进了 findings）

1. **F87 慢放不是缺陷**：前两轮我把「思考行没出现」「已判定 0 条」当成前端 bug，真因是驱动在**网络收流结束**（按钮恢复可用）时就去断言，而界面还在按慢放回放——服务端 1 秒跑完的一场，界面要放 15–20 秒。教训：一次任务有**两个终点**（数据的、界面的），断言界面就得等界面自己说追平。
2. **F88 固定调试端口会把测试对象换掉**：上一轮的 Edge 没随驱动退出，固定端口上的监听者还是它，新驱动连上去测的是**旧浏览器**（它 localStorage 里已记着「教程看过」，所以「首开自动弹教程」假失败）。修法：端口按进程号挑 + 开跑前自检「这是全新的配置」。同类事故第三次出现，所以改成由**启动检查**兜底，不再指望收尾一定成功。
3. **F89 离线回放的边界要写在按钮旁边（产品侧，已修）**：离线卡带按提示词指纹录，只录了「一键示例 → 全部采纳那几条建议」这一条路。评委自己补一句真话、或只采纳一部分建议再点「改完再跑一遍」，会撞上缺卡带的报错。不去录第二条路（改法组合会爆炸），而是在按钮旁事先说明：**「离线回放只录了演示这一条路…想试自己的改法就把模式切到「联网」」**（`RevisionBar` 新增 `rerunHint`，点测盯住这句话）。

```ts
 * 时间戳写死（1980-01-01），同一份输入永远产出逐字节相同的文件（ADR-0010 的确定性口径）。

import { deflateRawSync } from 'node:zlib';

const DOS_TIME = 0;
const DOS_DATE = 33; // 1980-01-01

export interface ZipFile { name: string; data: Buffer }

const CRC_TABLE = (() => { ... })();

export function crc32(bytes: Uint8Array): number {...}
function u16... u32...
export function zipArchive(files: readonly ZipFile[]): Buffer {...}
```

```ts
import { deflateRawSync } from "node:zlib";

/**
 * 极小的 ZIP 写入器：把一组「文件名 + 字节」打成一个 zip 容器。
 *
 * 为什么自己写：.xlsx 与 .docx 都是 zip 容器，而 Node 自带 zlib、不自带 zip 写入器。
 * 引一个 zip 库会多一个复现失败点（与 ADR-0007 砍掉 .doc 解析同一条纪律），
 * 而这里要的只有三件事：本地头 + deflate + 中央目录。
 *
 * 确定性：时间戳写死 1980-01-01（DOS 纪元的起点），同一份输入两次导出逐字节相同；
 * 否则每跑一次导出都会在版本库里留下一个「变了但其实没变」的文件。
 */

export interface ZipFile {
  name: string;
  data: Buffer;
}

/** 1980-01-01 00:00:00 的 DOS 时间戳：年 =(33>>9)+1980，月 =(33>>5)&15，日 =33&31 */
const DOS_TIME = 0;
const DOS_DATE = 33;

const CRC_TABLE = (() => {
  const table = new Uint32Array(256);
  for (let index = 0; index < table.length; index += 1) {
    let value = index;
    for (let bit = 0; bit < 8; bit += 1) {
      value = (value & 1) === 1 ? (0xedb88320 ^ (value >>> 1)) >>> 0 : value >>> 1;
    }
    table[index] = value >>> 0;
  }
  return table;
})();

export function crc32(bytes: Uint8Array): number {
  ...
}

function u16(...)...
function u32(...)...

export function zipArchive(files: readonly ZipFile[]): Buffer { ... }
```

Content of zip.ts (String.raw, with double quotes):

```
import { deflateRawSync } from "node:zlib";

/**
 * 极小的 ZIP 写入器：把一组「文件名 + 字节」打成 zip 容器。
 *
 * .xlsx 与 .docx 都是 zip 容器，而 Node 自带 zlib、不自带 zip 写入器。
 * 引一个 zip 库就多一个复现失败点（ADR-0007 砍掉老 .doc 解析是同一条纪律），
 * 而这里要的只有三件事：本地头、deflate、中央目录。
 *
 * 确定性：时间戳写死 1980-01-01（DOS 纪元的起点），同一份输入两次导出逐字节相同；
 * 否则每跑一次导出都会在版本库里留下一个「变了但其实没变」的文件。
 */

export interface ZipFile {
  name: string;
  data: Buffer;
}

/** 1980-01-01 00:00:00 的 DOS 时间戳：年 =(33>>9)+1980，月 =(33>>5)&15，日 =33&31 */
const DOS_TIME = 0;
const DOS_DATE = 33;

const CRC_TABLE = (() => {
  const table = new Uint32Array(256);
  for (let index = 0; index < 256; index += 1) {
    let value = index;
    for (let bit = 0; bit < 8; bit += 1) {
      value = (value & 1) === 1 ? (0xedb88320 ^ (value >>> 1)) >>> 0 : value >>> 1;
    }
    table[index] = value >>> 0;
  }
  return table;
})();

export function crc32(bytes: Uint8Array): number {
  let crc = 0xffffffff;
  for (let index = 0; index < bytes.length; index += 1) {
    const entry = CRC_TABLE[(crc ^ (bytes[index] ?? 0)) & 0xff] ?? 0;
    crc = (entry ^ (crc >>> 8)) >>> 0;
  }
  return (crc ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}

function u16(value: number): Buffer { ... }
function u32(value: number): Buffer { ... }

export function zipArchive(files: readonly ZipFile[]): Buffer { ...same as before... }
```

```
const lines = markdown.split(/\r?\n/);
let i = 0;
while (i < lines.length) {
  const raw = lines[i]; const line = raw.trim();
  if (line === '') { i++; continue; }
  if (/^#{1,3} /.test(line)) { level = count; text = line.slice(level+1).trim(); push heading; i++; continue; }
  if (/^[-*] /.test(line)) { collect consecutive bullets; push bullet each; continue; }
  if (/^> /.test(line)) { collect quote lines; push quote paragraphs; continue; }
  if (line.startsWith('|')) { collect table lines; push a paragraph per row: first cell bold + ' — ' + rest join ' / '; separator rows (===,---) skipped; continue; }
  // plain paragraph: collect until blank
  while ... push body paragraph
}
```

Body building: split text by NL → lines. For each line: if marks && spans → build runs from the line with marks. Simplest: for each line, filter spans intersecting the line, then walk the line.

Let me write:
```ts
function runsForLine(line: string, start: number, spans): DocxRun[] {
  const ordered = spans.filter(s => s.start < start + line.length && s.end > start && s.end > s.start).sort((a,b)=>a.start-b.start);
  if (ordered.length === 0) return [{ text: line }];
  const out = [];
  let cursor = start;
  const lineEnd = start + line.length;
  for (const span of ordered) {
    const from = Math.max(span.start, cursor); const to = Math.min(span.end, lineEnd);
    if (to <= from) continue;
    if (from > cursor) out.push({ text: text.slice(cursor, from) });
    out.push({ text: text.slice(from, to), color: '047857', highlight: 'green' });
    cursor = to;
  }
  if (cursor < lineEnd) out.push({ text: text.slice(cursor, lineEnd) });
  return out.length === 0 ? [{ text: line }] : out;
}
```
Where `text` is the whole body text (needed for slicing). Pass it in.

Then:
```ts
function docxBodyLines(text: string, spans): DocxParagraph[] {
  const out = [];
  let cursor = 0;
  for (const line of text.split(NL)) {
    out.push({ kind: 'body', runs: spans.length === 0 ? [{ text: line }] : runsForLine(line, cursor, text, spans) });
    cursor += line.length + 1;
  }
  return out;
}
```

```ts
/** 正文按行拆段：有改动区间就把改过的片段标绿高亮，其余照原样 */
function docxBody(text: string, spans: readonly Revision[]): DocxParagraph[] {
  const out: DocxParagraph[] = [];
  const ordered = spans
    .filter((item) => item.start >= 0 && item.end <= text.length && item.end > item.start)
    .slice()
    .sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  let cursor = 0;
  for (const line of text.split(NL)) {
    const lineEnd = cursor + line.length;
    const runs: DocxRun[] = [];
    let at = cursor;
    for (const span of ordered) {
      if (span.end <= at || span.start >= lineEnd) continue;
      const from = Math.max(span.start, at);
      const to = Math.min(span.end, lineEnd);
      if (to <= from) continue;
      if (from > at) runs.push({ text: text.slice(at, from) });
      runs.push({ text: text.slice(from, to), color: '047857', highlight: 'green' });
      at = to;
    }
    if (runs.length === 0) runs.push({ text: line });
    else if (at < lineEnd) runs.push({ text: text.slice(at, lineEnd) });
    out.push({ kind: 'body', runs });
    cursor = lineEnd + 1;
  }
  return out;
}

function docxChangeList(view: SessionView, revised: RevisedView): DocxParagraph[] {
  const out: DocxParagraph[] = [];
  out.push(headingParagraph('本版改了什么（' + String(revised.applied.length) + ' 处）', 3));
  if (revised.applied.length === 0) out.push(noteParagraph('没有改动，这一份就是原文。'));
  for (const item of revised.applied) {
    const claim = view.claims.find((row) => row.id === item.claimId);
    const why = claim === undefined || claim.suggestion === null ? '' : claim.suggestion.why;
    if (item.before.length === 0) {
      out.push(bulletParagraph('新增一行：' + item.after.replace(/^[\r\n]+/, '')));
      out.push(noteParagraph('这条主张在简历里没有原句可改，这句是你自己补的事实。'));
      continue;
    }
    out.push({ kind: 'bullet', runs: [{ text: '改前：' + item.before, strike: true, color: GRAY }] });
    out.push({ kind: 'bullet', runs: [{ text: '改后：' + item.after, color: '047857' }] });
    if (why !== '') out.push(noteParagraph('为什么：' + why));
  }
  if (revised.pending.length > 0) out.push(noteParagraph('还有 ' + String(revised.pending.length) + ' 条建议没有采纳（' + revised.pending.join('、') + '）：这份简历里保留了原话。'));
  for (const item of revised.skipped) out.push(noteParagraph(item.claimId + ' 没能改：' + item.reason));
  return out;
}

/**
 * 渲染一份真正的 .docx（Word 能直接打开、能继续改）。
 * 正文与 HTML 版同源：同一个 text、同一份 spans，两边的差异只有呈现层。
 */
export function renderResumeDocx(view: SessionView, revised: RevisedView | null, options: ResumeHtmlOptions = {}): Buffer {
  const tailored = options.tailored ?? null;
  const text = tailored !== null ? tailored.text : revised === null ? view.session.resumeText : revised.text;
  const marks = options.marks === true && revised !== null && tailored === null;
  const title = view.session.jobTitle === '' ? '简历' : view.session.jobTitle;
  const subtitle = tailored !== null
    ? '按《' + tailored.jdTitle + '》筛出的一版：留下 ' + String(tailored.keptSegments) + ' 段、去掉 ' + String(tailored.droppedSegments) + ' 段；原文一个字都没有改'
    : revised === null
      ? '原文导出于 CrossExam 质询室'
      : '依据质询结果修订（' + String(revised.applied.length) + ' 处改动）';
  const paragraphs: DocxParagraph[] = [titleParagraph(title), subtitleParagraph(subtitle)];
  for (const item of docxBody(text, marks && revised !== null ? revised.spans : [])) paragraphs.push(item);
  if (marks && revised !== null) for (const item of docxChangeList(view, revised)) paragraphs.push(item);
  return writeDocx(paragraphs);
}
```

```ts
      'content-disposition': 'attachment; filename="' + reportDocxFileName(view) + '"',
    });
  });
```

- 代码：
```js
const fs = await import('node:fs');
function patch(path, pairs) {
  let src = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
  for (const [oldText, newText, label] of pairs) {
    if (!src.includes(oldText)) throw new Error(path + ' 找不到锚点：' + label);
    src = src.split(oldText).join(newText);
  }
  fs.writeFileSync(path, src);
  return src.length;
}
```

Careful: reading res.text() after arrayBuffer() — the body may be consumed. In Hono's test app.request, the response body can be read once. So do text first then arrayBuffer? Both consume. Better: clone? Simpler: read arrayBuffer once, then use Buffer.from(bytes).toString('utf8') to check content (entity-escaped text will still appear: '跟' plain characters appear in document.xml). Since zip is deflated, the text may be compressed and not visible... With deflate, the XML is compressed so plain text won't appear in the raw bytes! In the unrelated detail: zipArchive uses deflate when it reduces size. So searching raw bytes for the Chinese text may fail.

```text
52|    if (readU32(buffer, i) === SIG_EOCD) return i;
```

```js
const fs = await import('node:fs');
function patch(path, pairs) {
  let src = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
  for (const [oldText, newText, label] of pairs) {
    if (!src.includes(oldText)) throw new Error(path + ' 缺锚点：' + label);
    src = src.split(oldText).join(newText);
  }
  fs.writeFileSync(path, src);
  console.log('patched', path.split('/').slice(-2).join('/'), '->', src.length);
}
```

```ts
/**
 * 真 .docx 写入器（D:/CZB/packages/server/src/docx.ts）。
 *
 * 为什么这一层值得单独测：导出的文件要拿 Word 打开，错了就是「用户双击后看到
 * 「文件已损坏」」，而且这种事永远不会在浏览器里出现。所以断言落在容器结构上：
 * zip 魔数、Word 要求的几份部件、正文 XML 里的文字与转义。
 */

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { readZip, readZipEntry } from '../src/import/zip.ts';
import {
  bodyParagraph,
  bulletParagraph,
  headingParagraph,
  markdownParagraphs,
  quoteParagraph,
  titleParagraph,
  writeDocx,
} from '../src/docx.ts';

const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);

function textOf(buffer: Uint8Array, name: string): string {
  const entry = readZipEntry(buffer, name);
  expect(entry).not.toBeNull();
  return Buffer.from(entry ?? new Uint8Array()).toString('utf8');
}

describe('docx 写入器', () => {
  it('同一批段落写两遍逐字节相同', () => {
    const paragraphs = [titleParagraph('质询报告'), bodyParagraph('正文一句')];
    const first = writeDocx(paragraphs);
    const second = writeDocx(paragraphs);
    expect(first.equals(second)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('容器里是 zip，且 Word 要的五份部件都在', () => {
    const buffer = writeDocx([bodyParagraph('你好')]);
    expect(buffer[0]).toBe(0x50);
    expect(buffer[1]).toBe(0x4b);
    const names = readZip(new Uint8Array(buffer)).map((e) => e.name).sort();
    expect(names).toEqual(['[Content_Types].xml', '_rels/.rels', 'word/_rels/document.xml.rels', 'word/document.xml', 'word/styles.xml'].sort());
  });

  it('段落里的中英文都进了正文部件，段落数对得上', () => {
    const document = textOf(new Uint8Array(writeDocx([titleParagraph('质询报告'), bodyParagraph('张三 · 后端工程师')])), 'word/document.xml');
    expect(document).toContain('质询报告');
    expect(document).toContain('张三 · 后端工程师');
    expect((document.match(/<w:p>/g) ?? []).length).toBe(2);
  });

  it('XML 特殊字符被转义，不会把文件写坏', () => {
    const document = textOf(new Uint8Array(writeDocx([bodyParagraph('a < b & c > d "e"')])), 'word/document.xml');
    expect(document).toContain('a &lt; b &amp; c &gt; d');
    expect(document).not.toContain('a < b');
  });

  it('一行都没有时也是一个能打开的 Word 文件', () => {
    const buffer = writeDocx([]);
    expect(textOf(new Uint8Array(buffer), 'word/document.xml')).toContain('<w:body>');
  });

  it('Markdown 认得标题、项目符号、引用与表格行', () => {
    const markdown = [
      '# 报告',
      '## 一、主张',
      '### 小标题',
      '- 第一条',
      '> 一句引用',
      '| 主张 | 判定 |',
      '| --- | --- |',
      '| C1 | 站得住 |',
      '**加粗**的一句',
    ].join(NL);
    const paragraphs = markdownParagraphs(markdown);
    expect(paragraphs.map((p) => p.kind)).toEqual(['title', 'heading', 'heading', 'bullet', 'quote', 'body', 'body']);
    expect(paragraphs[1]?.level).toBe(2);
    expect(paragraphs[2]?.level).toBe(3);
    // 表格行被压成一句：首格加粗 + 「：」+ 其余用 / 连接（Word 里没有等宽表格，宁可读起来顺）
    const table = paragraphs[5]?.runs.map((r) => r.text).join('');
    expect(table).toBe('主张：判定 / 站得住');
    const bold = paragraphs[6]?.runs;
    expect(bold?.length).toBe(1);
    expect(bold?.[0]?.text).toBe('加粗');
    expect(bold?.[0]?.bold).toBe(true);
  });

  it('表格的分隔行被跳过，不留一行破折号', () => {
    const paragraphs = markdownParagraphs(['| A | B |', '| --- | --- |', '| 1 | 2 |'].join(NL));
    expect(paragraphs.length).toBe(2);
  });
});
```

Then '| C1 | 站得住 |' → 'C1：站得住'. paragraphs[5] is index 5: paragraphs list indices: 0 title, 1 heading(一), 2 heading(小标题), 3 bullet, 4 quote, 5 table row '主张：判定', 6 'C1：站得住', 7 '加粗的一句'. Now patch e2e. For docx checks I need to fetch bytes and inspect: e2e uses global fetch (Node 22+, has ArrayBuffer). Assert PK magic via `new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer())`. Also can decompress? e2e.mjs could import readZipEntry from the server src? It's a Node script in tools; importing ../packages/server/src/import/zip.ts (TS) from Node 24 — type stripping might work only for .ts files when running with node (Node 24 strips types by default for .ts). The e2e script itself is .mjs; importing a .ts module from .mjs — Node's type stripping applies to .ts files loaded by Node, it should work (experimental but Node 24 enables by default). Risky. Simpler: assert PK magic + content-disposition + openxml type, and additionally check the raw bytes contain the uncompressed file name 'word/document.xml' (zip stores names uncompressed in local file headers). That's a decent structural check without needing inflate. And for content text I can't easily check. But e2e already checks resume txt content. Fine.

```js
const reportDoc = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + hrefOf('doc'));
const reportDocBytes = new Uint8Array(await reportDoc.arrayBuffer());
const reportDocRaw = Buffer.from(reportDocBytes).toString('latin1');
check('Word 入口给的是真 .docx：zip 容器 + Word 部件 + openxml 类型',
  reportDoc.status === 200
    && String(reportDoc.headers.get('content-type')).indexOf('openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document') >= 0
    && String(reportDoc.headers.get('content-disposition')).indexOf('.docx') >= 0
    && reportDocBytes[0] === 0x50 && reportDocBytes[1] === 0x4b
    && reportDocRaw.indexOf('word/document.xml') >= 0,
  String(reportDocBytes.length) + ' 字节');
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

I'll add named styles so Word shows headings in the navigation pane (and TOC works), which is a genuine quality upgrade for a "真 Word 文件" deliverable:

In STYLES_XML add:
- Title style (styleId "Title", name "Title")? Word maps "Title" style. Use styleId "Title", w:name "Title", basedOn Normal, next Normal, rPr bold 16pt, pPr spacing after 80.
- Heading1/Heading2/Heading3 (styleId "Heading1"/"Heading2"/"Heading3", name "heading 1".."heading 3" — Word's built-in names are "heading 1"; using <w:name w:val="heading 1"/> and w:styleId "Heading1" makes it a built-in mapped style so navigation pane recognizes it).
- Quote style (styleId "Quote", name "Quote").
- Subtitle? Use styleId "Subtitle", name "Subtitle".

Because built-in style IDs need w:basedOn Normal and w:qFormat maybe. Minimal definition works in Word.

Then paragraphXml should emit `<w:pStyle w:val="Heading2"/>` etc. Note: run-level rPr can still override; but if I use pStyle with rPr in style, Word uses the style's formatting. Simpler: keep explicit run formatting too (belt and suspenders) — harmless duplication but the pStyle gives outline level. Actually duplication makes styles feel "not real" if a user changes Heading 1 style and nothing happens. Better to move size/bold into the styles and leave runs plain for headings/title/quote/subtitle. But cautious: if Word doesn't apply styles (weird readers), text would lose formatting. python-docx will report style names. Hmm.

Middle ground that is genuinely correct: define the styles (with bold/size/color) AND reference them via pStyle; remove the corresponding direct run overrides for those paragraph kinds. Word will render style-based formatting, and users can restyle. Risk: if the style definition is subtly wrong, headings lose bold. I'll validate with python-docx by checking `paragraph.style.name` and run.bold for headings.

Also outline level: built-in heading styles have outlineLvl from Word defaults; but we define our own style with styleId Heading1 and name "heading 1" — Word treats it as the built-in heading style (mapped by styleId), and TOC/navigation uses styleId/outlineLvl. To be safe, add `<w:outlineLvl w:val="0"/>` to Heading1's pPr (0/1/2), which is exactly what makes the navigation pane work.

Implementation plan in docx.ts:
1. Add `pStyle(styleId)` in paragraphXml props: title → "Title", subtitle → "Subtitle", heading → "Heading" + level, quote → "Quote", bullet/body → none.
2. Build STYLES_XML with helper `styleXml(id, name, basedOn, rPr, pPr)`.
3. Keep run-level formatting for inline emphasis (bold, color, strike, highlight, size for notes) but drop redundant bold/size for title/subtitle/heading/quote since styles carry it? Keep it simple: keep run formatting as-is (it stays consistent), but ensure styles exist with the same values so restyling roughly works and outline works. Hmm, "roughly" is exactly the F-style sloppiness I should avoid.

Decision: move the kind-level formatting (size/bold/color/spacing) into styles; runs for those kinds carry only inline extras. This is the cleaner design and testable: assert that document.xml has `<w:pStyle w:val="Heading2"/>` and that headingParagraph('x').runs[0] has no size override? Hmm, but then helpers must not set size. Test asserts python-docx reports style "Heading 2" — I'll validate that with the real reader.

Wait — risk: markdownParagraphs uses headingParagraph for `#` level 1; report's `## 一、` → level 2; test expects levels. Fine.

But titleParagraph is used for cover title; Subtitle style.

Implement:

styles.xml content:
- docDefaults (unchanged)
- Normal style
- Title: name "Title", basedOn Normal, next Normal, pPr spacing after 80 line 280 auto, rPr rFonts + b + sz 32
- Subtitle: name "Subtitle", basedOn Normal, pPr spacing after 240, rPr color 6b7280 sz 19
- Heading1: name "heading 1", basedOn Normal, next Normal, pPr outlineLvl 0 + spacing before 260 after 100 + keepNext, rPr b + sz 28
- Heading2: ... outlineLvl 1, sz 24
- Heading3: ... outlineLvl 2, sz 22
- Quote: name "Quote", basedOn Normal, pPr ind left 420 spacing before 60 after 60, rPr color 6b7280

Note sz values are half-points: title 16pt→32, subtitle 9.5→19, h1 14→28, h2 12→24, h3 11→22.

Run-level: for kinds with a style, don't pass size/bold/color from helpers. But the tests I wrote: `expect(paragraphs[7]?.runs[0]?.bold).toBe(true)` for inline **bold** — that's inline, unaffected.

Also runXml always writes rFonts + sz (defaulting to BODY_SIZE) — that overrides the style's sz! Because run-level sz takes precedence over paragraph style. So to let styles carry size, runXml must omit sz when run.size is undefined. But docDefaults already sets 10.5pt, so omitting is safe. Similarly color: only when specified. rFonts: keep (harmless, same font as docDefaults) or omit when not specified? /** 段落级样式 ID：标题类走 Word 内置样式，导航窗格与目录才认得出来 */
```ts
function styleIdOf(paragraph: DocxParagraph): string | null {
  const kind = paragraph.kind ?? "body";
  if (kind === "title") return "Title";
  if (kind === "subtitle") return "Subtitle";
  if (kind === "heading") return "Heading" + String(paragraph.level ?? 2);
  if (kind === "quote") return "Quote";
  return null;
}
```

```ts
function paragraphXml(paragraph: DocxParagraph): string {
  const kind = paragraph.kind ?? "body";
  const props: string[] = [];
  const styleId = styleIdOf(paragraph);
  if (styleId !== null) props.push("<w:pStyle" + attr("w:val", styleId) + "/>");
  // 只有没有样式的段落才在这里写版式：标题的间距与字号都归样式管，
  // 用户在 Word 里改「标题 2」，整份文档跟着变。
  if (kind === "bullet") props.push("<w:spacing" + attr("w:before", "40") + attr("w:after", "40") + attr("w:line", "280") + attr("w:lineRule", "auto") + "/><w:ind" + attr("w:left", "420") + attr("w:hanging", "220") + "/>");
  else if (kind === "body") props.push("<w:spacing" + attr("w:before", "0") + attr("w:after", "80") + attr("w:line", "280") + attr("w:lineRule", "auto") + "/>");
  const runs = paragraph.runs.map(runXml).join("");
  return "<w:p><w:pPr>" + props.join("") + "</w:pPr>" + runs + "</w:p>";
}
```

STYLES_XML new:
```ts
function styleXml(id: string, name: string, options: { bold?: boolean; size?: number; color?: string; outline?: number; before?: number; after?: number; indent?: number; keepNext?: boolean } = {}): string {
  const rPr: string[] = ["<w:rFonts" + attr("w:ascii", "Calibri") + attr("w:hAnsi", "Calibri") + attr("w:eastAsia", BODY_FONT) + "/>"];
  if (options.bold === true) rPr.push("<w:b/>");
  if (options.color !== undefined) rPr.push("<w:color" + attr("w:val", options.color) + "/>");
  if (options.size !== undefined) {
    rPr.push("<w:sz" + attr("w:val", String(Math.round(options.size * 2))) + "/>");
    rPr.push("<w:szCs" + attr("w:val", String(Math.round(options.size * 2))) + "/>");
  }
  const pPr: string[] = [];
  const spacing: string[] = [];
  if (options.before !== undefined) spacing.push(attr("w:before", String(options.before)));
  if (options.after !== undefined) spacing.push(attr("w:after", String(options.after)));
  spacing.push(attr("w:line", "280"), attr("w:lineRule", "auto"));
  pPr.push("<w:spacing" + spacing.join("") + "/>");
  if (options.indent !== undefined) pPr.push("<w:ind" + attr("w:left", String(options.indent)) + "/>");
  if (options.keepNext === true) pPr.push("<w:keepNext/>");
  if (options.outline !== undefined) pPr.push("<w:outlineLvl" + attr("w:val", String(options.outline)) + "/>");
  return "<w:style" + attr("w:type", "paragraph") + attr("w:styleId", id) + "><w:name" + attr("w:val", name) + "/><w:basedOn" + attr("w:val", "Normal") + "/><w:next" + attr("w:val", "Normal") + "/><w:pPr>" + pPr.join("") + "</w:pPr><w:rPr>" + rPr.join("") + "</w:rPr></w:style>";
}
```
Careful: `attr` escapes, fine. Note spacing requires order? OOXML w:spacing attributes order irrelevant.

STYLES_XML = XML_HEAD + "<w:styles xmlns:w=...>" + docDefaults + normal + styles + "</w:styles>".

While e2e runs, write the docs. Let me draft:

1. `docs/adr/0026-word-export-is-a-real-docx.md` — decisions:
   1. 真 .docx：zip 容器 + 五份固定 XML，自己写，不引依赖（与 xlsx 同一条纪律；zip 写入器抽到 zip.ts 共用）。
   2. 段落挂 Word 内置样式（Title/Subtitle/Heading1-3/Quote + outlineLvl），字号与粗体归样式管，行内不重复写。
   3. Markdown 只认报告实际会产出的形状；表格压成一句（首格加粗 + ：+ / 连接），不引完整 Markdown 语义。
   4. 简历/一岗一版的 .docx 保留改动标记（改前删除线灰、改后绿底），与 HTML 版同一份事实。
   5. 确定性：zip 时间戳写死 1980-01-01，同一输入逐字节相同。
   6. 三条路由同样统一 openxml content-type，旧的 /report.doc 等直接消失（不做兼容跳转：这是本地单人应用，没有外部消费者）。

2. `findings.md` F90/F91:
   - F90: 「真 .docx」的验收标准不是后缀也不是「双击能开」——Word 打开一个 HTML 改名文件也能开。判定标准必须是容器结构 + 部件存在 + 段落挂的是内置样式；用第三方读取器（python-docx）打开并读 style 名，是最省事的外部证据（自己写的断言只能证明自己写了什么）。
   - F91: 直接格式会把样式钉死：第一版给每个标题都写了 sz/bold，段落又挂 Heading 样式 → 用户在 Word 里改「标题 2」没有任何反应，样式只是标签。字号/粗体必须归样式管，行内只在真正需要强调时写。相关：F65（旋钮改的是哪一层）同族。

ADR-0026: 真 .docx：Word 打开的不是网页

决策:
1. 导出 Word 一律出真 .docx（OOXML 容器 + 五份部件），不再出「HTML 改后缀 .doc」。
2. 自己写写入器（packages/server/src/docx.ts），不引第三方库（与 ADR-0007 砍老 .doc、ADR-0025 自写 xlsx 同一条依赖纪律）。
3. zip 写入器抽成独立模块 zip.ts，xlsx 复用它（crc32 / zipArchive / DOS 时间戳写死 1980-01-01）。
4. 段落挂 Word 内置样式（Title/Subtitle/Heading1-3/Quote），不是只写直接格式；字号只由样式给，run 只在明确要求时才写 sz。
5. 三条导出路由改名：/report.docx、/resume.docx、/tailored.docx，MIME application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document。
6. Markdown→段落映射是保守的一个函数 markdownParagraphs（#/##/### → Heading1-3、- → bullet、> → quote、表格行压成一行文字），不用它做版式还原。
7. 验收标准是「换一个不是自己写的读取器能认出结构」：python-docx 打开读样式名（report 的标题 → Heading 1）——这条进测试与录前核对。

取舍:
- 不做页眉页脚/分页符/图片/批注（清单里曾写「导出 Word 带批注」，明确不做）。
- Word 里改完再导回来不解析（写入器只写不读）。
- 报告与简历仍是同一份事实两种呈现，docx 只换呈现，不重算判定。
- 老 .doc 后缀不再生成，命中旧路径会 404（测试里 404 循环已改）。

F90: 真文件与「换后缀」的判定标准是「另一个读取器」——自己写的写入器 + 自己的测试只能证明自洽；用 python-docx 打开并打印样式名，才证明容器与部件是真的（同「跨实现验证」范式；此前 PDF 用 Edge 打印、xlsx 用 CRC 公认向量也是同一类外部锚点）。

F91: 结构不能只写在直接格式里 —— 只写 w:sz/w:b 时 Word 导航窗格与「标题 2」样式改不动（215 段全是「正文」）；改用内置样式（pStyle + styles.xml）后导航窗格、目录、样式修改才成立。表现是「文件能打开、排版看着对，但结构是假的」——比打不开更难发现。

Also F91 note: 字号写进每个 run 会把样式钉死（runXml 原来无脑写 sz）。

```js
const BT = String.fromCharCode(96);
```

```js
const msgs = {
  m1: b(`feat(server): 导出的 Word 是真 .docx，不是改后缀的网页

在此之前「导出 Word」给的是同一份 HTML 换个 .doc 后缀：Word 打得开，
但段落是 div、结构是假的。现在自写一个最小的 OOXML 写入器：
段落挂 Word 内置样式（Title / Subtitle / Heading1-3 / Quote），
字号交给 styles.xml，run 只在明确要求时才写 w:sz——否则会把样式钉死。

不引第三方库的理由与砍掉老 .doc 解析、自写 .xlsx 是同一条：
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Compressed. Now proceed with requirement ②: desktop app + left-sidebar multi-page layout + multi-step onboarding wizard.

Let me plan concretely.

Design (P15):
1. **Pages (left sidebar nav)**: 
   - 首页（Home / 概览）: 一句话说明 + 「开始质询」主按钮 + 最近一场摘要 + 三步上手 + 提示卡。
   - 投料台（Intake）: 材料输入（模板、简历、JD、面试官、模式、难度、风格）+ 开始质询。
   - 质询台（Interview）: StageRail + 实时输出 + 主张与判定 + 岗位对照 + 反问（中栏三块 + 实时） + 右栏原文/采纳/比上次/一岗一版。
   - 报告（Report）: 报告导出 + 台账 + 打包 + 报告内容预览（iframe? 或链接）+ 成长档案。
   - 历史（History）: 会话列表 + 仪表盘 + 版本链。
   - 设置（Settings）: 配色、模式（live/replay）、模型、节奏、新手教程、启动器/桌面端说明。
2. Keep all panels but条件渲染 by page. Sidebar with icons/labels + active highlight; data-shot="nav-<page>".
3. Multi-step onboarding wizard: 首开自动弹的向导（4-5 步：欢迎 → 选行业模板/上传简历 → 选面试官与强度 → 开始质询 → 看报告），可跳过。Existing HelpPanel is a tutorial panel; the wizard is a separate overlay. Per user: "多步新手向导" — implement `OnboardingWizard.tsx` with steps and 下一步/跳过 buttons, first-open auto (key `crossexam.seen-wizard`), each step can jump to the corresponding page.
4. Desktop packaging: extend launcher to open Edge in `--app=http://127.0.0.1:PORT` mode (no address bar) + create desktop shortcut with icon. Zero deps.

Concerns: e2e (165 assertions) depends on the single page layout: all data-shot elements must remain reachable. If I split into pages, e2e's `hasShot`/`clickShot` will fail for elements on other pages. So the page shell must be navigable by the e2e script, and I must update e2e to navigate pages. That's a big e2e refactor (165 assertions).

Better approach to limit risk: **keep the elements mounted but on separate "pages" rendered via CSS visibility?** No — hiding via CSS keeps them in DOM (hasShot works with querySelector... but `textOf` requires visibility? e2e reads innerText — hidden elements return empty innerText). Hmm.

Alternative pragmatic approach that satisfies the user's ask while minimizing breakage: implement the sidebar multi-page layout, and update e2e to click nav before asserting sections. e2e is our own tool; I can add a helper `gotoPage(id)` and insert navigation calls at section boundaries. That's doable: the e2e script has ~12 sections (A..L) mapping roughly to pages.

Given the scale and remaining context, I should proceed step by step:

Step 1: Write design doc docs/design/p15-review.md (short).
Step 2: Implement pages shell in web: new component `SideNav.tsx`, page state in App.tsx, restructure render into page components (maybe keep inline JSX with conditionals).
Step 3: Onboarding wizard component.
Step 4: Desktop launcher app-mode + shortcut.
Step 5: Update e2e with page navigation; run e2e; update tests (state/help tests).
Step 6: Docs, screenshots, PDF, commit.

That's a lot of work. Let's start.

First, design doc. Then look at launcher code and help.ts to plan. Let me read tools/launcher files and HelpPanel to reuse patterns.

Let me read: tools/launcher/launch.mjs, tools/launcher/src/plan.mjs, packages/web/src/components/HelpPanel.tsx (for overlay pattern), and help.ts (HELP_STEPS).

Also check index.css for nav styles/pane classes.

Let me batch reads.
